module event_counter
(clk,
resetn,
stats_reset,
incr,
count);
input clk;
input resetn;
input stats_reset;
input incr;
output [31:0]count;
wire \<const1> ;
wire clk;
wire [31:0]count;
wire [31:0]count0;
wire count_i_n_0;
wire incr;
wire resetn;
wire stats_reset;
VCC VCC
(.P(\<const1> ));
RTL_ADD10 count0_i
(.I0(count),
.I1(\<const1> ),
.O(count0));
RTL_MUX count_i
(.I0(\<const1> ),
.I1(stats_reset),
.O(count_i_n_0),
.S(resetn));
RTL_REG_SYNC__BREG_1 \count_reg[0]
(.C(clk),
.CE(incr),
.D(count0[0]),
.Q(count[0]),
.RST(count_i_n_0));
RTL_REG_SYNC__BREG_1 \count_reg[10]
(.C(clk),
.CE(incr),
.D(count0[10]),
.Q(count[10]),
.RST(count_i_n_0));
RTL_REG_SYNC__BREG_1 \count_reg[11]
(.C(clk),
.CE(incr),
.D(count0[11]),
.Q(count[11]),
.RST(count_i_n_0));
RTL_REG_SYNC__BREG_1 \count_reg[12]
(.C(clk),
.CE(incr),
.D(count0[12]),
.Q(count[12]),
.RST(count_i_n_0));
RTL_REG_SYNC__BREG_1 \count_reg[13]
(.C(clk),
.CE(incr),
.D(count0[13]),
.Q(count[13]),
.RST(count_i_n_0));

(.C(clk),
.CE
